{% load static %}
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="tr">
<head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8">
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
    <title>{% block title %}Şirket Yönetim Paneli{% endblock %}</title>
    <link rel="stylesheet" href="{% static 'yonetim/css/style.css' %}">
</head>
<body>
    <div class="layout">
        <aside class="sidebar">
            <div class="sidebar-brand">
                Şirket<span>Panel</span>
            </div>
            <nav class="sidebar-nav">
                <a href="{% url 'yonetim:finans_portfoy' %}" class="{% if active_menu == 'portfoy' %}active{% endif %}">Finansal Portföy</a>
                <a href="{% url 'yonetim:cari_liste' %}" class="{% if active_menu == 'cariler' %}active{% endif %}">Cari Hesaplar</a>
                <a href="{% url 'yonetim:kasa_liste' %}" class="{% if active_menu == 'kasalar' %}active{% endif %}">Kasa & Banka</a>
                <a href="{% url 'yonetim:gider_liste' %}" class="{% if active_menu == 'giderler' %}active{% endif %}">İşletme Giderleri</a>
                <a href="{% url 'yonetim:fatura_liste' %}" class="{% if active_menu == 'faturalar' %}active{% endif %}">Faturalar</a>
                <a href="{% url 'yonetim:ceksenet_liste' %}" class="{% if active_menu == 'ceksenet' %}active{% endif %}">Çek & Senet</a>
                <a href="{% url 'yonetim:odeme_liste' %}" class="{% if active_menu == 'odemeler' %}active{% endif %}">Ödemeler</a>
                <a href="{% url 'yonetim:alacak_liste' %}" class="{% if active_menu == 'alacaklar' %}active{% endif %}">Alacaklar</a>
                <a href="{% url 'yonetim:haftalik_plan_liste' %}" class="{% if active_menu == 'planlar' %}active{% endif %}">Haftalık Takvim</a>
                <a href="{% url 'yonetim:stok_liste' %}" class="{% if active_menu == 'stoklar' %}active{% endif %}">Stoklar</a>
                <a href="{% url 'yonetim:butce_liste' %}" class="{% if active_menu == 'butce' %}active{% endif %}">Bütçe</a>
                <a href="{% url 'yonetim:vergi_ayarlari' %}" class="{% if active_menu == 'vergilendirme' %}active{% endif %}">Vergilendirme</a>
                <a href="{% url 'yonetim:fis_liste' %}" class="{% if active_menu == 'fisler' %}active{% endif %}">Fişler</a>
            </nav>
        </aside>

        <div class="main">
            <header class="main-header">
                <h1>{% block page_title %}Panel{% endblock %}</h1>
                {% block header_actions %}{% endblock %}
            </header>

            <main class="main-content">
                {% if messages %}
                <div class="messages">
                    {% for message in messages %}
                    <div class="message message-{{ message.tags }}">{{ message }}</div>
                    {% endfor %}
                </div>
                {% endif %}

                {% block content %}{% endblock %}
            </main>
        </div>
    </div>
</body>
</html>

```
{% extends 'yonetim/base.html' %}

{% block title %}Haftalık Takvim | Şirket Yönetim Paneli{% endblock %}
{% block page_title %}Haftalık Takvim{% endblock %}

{% block header_actions %}
<a href="{% url 'yonetim:haftalik_plan_ekle' %}" class="btn btn-primary">Yeni Plan Ekle</a>
{% endblock %}

{% block content %}
<div class="card">
    <div class="card-header calendar-header">
        <div class="calendar-nav">
            <a href="?baslangic={{ onceki_hafta|date:'Y-m-d' }}" class="btn btn-secondary">← Önceki Hafta</a>
            <span class="calendar-range">
                {{ hafta_baslangic|date:"d.m.Y" }} – {{ hafta_bitis|date:"d.m.Y" }}
            </span>
            <a href="?baslangic={{ sonraki_hafta|date:'Y-m-d' }}" class="btn btn-secondary">Sonraki Hafta →</a>
        </div>
        <a href="?baslangic={{ bugun|date:'Y-m-d' }}" class="btn btn-secondary">Bu Hafta</a>
    </div>
    <div class="card-body">
        <div class="calendar-grid">
            {% for gun in gunler %}
            <div class="calendar-day {% if gun.bugun_mu %}calendar-day-today{% endif %}">
                <div class="calendar-day-header">
                    <span class="calendar-day-name">{{ gun.isim }}</span>
                    <span class="calendar-day-date">{{ gun.tarih|date:"d.m" }}</span>
                </div>
                <div class="calendar-day-body">
                    {% for plan in gun.planlar %}
                    <div class="plan-card {% if plan.durum == 'tamamlandi' %}plan-card-done{% endif %}">
                        <div class="plan-card-title">{{ plan.baslik }}</div>
                        {% if plan.detay %}
                        <div class="plan-card-detail">{{ plan.detay|truncatechars:70 }}</div>
                        {% endif %}
                        <div class="plan-card-footer">
                            {% if plan.durum == 'tamamlandi' %}
                            <span class="badge badge-success">Tamamlandı</span>
                            {% else %}
                            <span class="badge badge-warning">Bekliyor</span>
                            {% endif %}
                            <form method="post" action="{% url 'yonetim:haftalik_plan_durum_degistir' plan.id %}" class="plan-card-form">
                                {% csrf_token %}
                                <button type="submit" class="btn btn-mini">
                                    {% if plan.durum == 'tamamlandi' %}Geri Al{% else %}Tamamla{% endif %}
                                </button>
                            </form>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    {% empty %}
                    <div class="calendar-empty">—</div>
                    {% endfor %}
                </div>
            </div>
            {% endfor %}
        </div>
    </div>
</div>
{% endblock %}
```

yonetim/templates/yonetim/finans_portfoy.html
```
{% extends 'yonetim/base.html' %}

{% block title %}Finansal Portföy | Şirket Yönetim Paneli{% endblock %}
{% block page_title %}Finansal Portföy{% endblock %}

{% block content %}
<div class="summary-grid">
    <div class="summary-card summary-primary">
        <span class="summary-label">Toplam Kasa & Banka</span>
        <span class="summary-value">{{ toplam_kasa|floatformat:2 }} ₺</span>
    </div>
    <div class="summary-card summary-success">
        <span class="summary-label">Cari Alacak</span>
        <span class="summary-value">{{ toplam_alacak|floatformat:2 }} ₺</span>
    </div>
    <div class="summary-card summary-danger">
        <span class="summary-label">Cari Borç</span>
        <span class="summary-value">{{ toplam_borc|floatformat:2 }} ₺</span>
    </div>
    <div class="summary-card {% if operasyonel_kar >= 0 %}summary-success{% else %}summary-danger{% endif %}">
        <span class="summary-label">Operasyonel Kâr / Zarar</span>
        <span class="summary-value">{{ operasyonel_kar|floatformat:2 }} ₺</span>
    </div>
</div>

<div class="dashboard-grid">
    <div class="card">
        <div class="card-header">
            <h2>Cari Denge</h2>
        </div>
        <div class="card-body">
            <table class="calc-table">
                <tr>
                    <td>Toplam Alacak</td>
                    <td class="text-success">{{ toplam_alacak|floatformat:2 }} ₺</td>
                </tr>
                <tr>
                    <td>Toplam Borç</td>
                    <td class="text-danger">{{ toplam_borc|floatformat:2 }} ₺</td>
                </tr>
                <tr class="calc-total">
                    <td>Net Denge</td>
                    <td class="{% if net_cari >= 0 %}text-success{% else %}text-danger{% endif %}">
                        {{ net_cari|floatformat:2 }} ₺
                    </td>
                </tr>
            </table>
        </div>
    </div>

    <div class="card">
        <div class="card-header">
            <h2>Bekleyen Çek / Senet</h2>
        </div>
        <div class="card-body">
            <table class="calc-table">
                <tr>
                    <td>Alınan (Portföyde)</td>
                    <td class="text-success">{{ bekleyen_alinan|floatformat:2 }} ₺</td>
                </tr>
                <tr>
                    <td>Verilen (Portföyde)</td>
                    <td class="text-danger">{{ bekleyen_verilen|floatformat:2 }} ₺</td>
                </tr>
                <tr class="calc-total">
                    <td>Net</td>
                    <td class="{% if net_ceksenet >= 0 %}text-success{% else %}text-danger{% endif %}">
                        {{ net_ceksenet|floatformat:2 }} ₺
                    </td>
                </tr>
            </table>
        </div>
    </div>

    <div class="card">
        <div class="card-header">
            <h2>Operasyonel Kâr / Zarar</h2>
        </div>
        <div class="card-body">
            <table class="calc-table">
                <tr>
                    <td>Gelen Faturalar</td>
                    <td class="text-success">{{ toplam_gelen_fatura|floatformat:2 }} ₺</td>
                </tr>
                <tr>
                    <td>Giden Faturalar</td>
                    <td class="text-danger">{{ toplam_giden_fatura|floatformat:2 }} ₺</td>
                </tr>
                <tr>
                    <td>İşletme Giderleri</td>
                    <td class="text-danger">{{ toplam_gider|floatformat:2 }} ₺</td>
                </tr>
                <tr class="calc-total">
                    <td>Net Kâr / Zarar</td>
                    <td class="{% if operasyonel_kar >= 0 %}text-success{% else %}text-danger{% endif %}">
                        {{ operasyonel_kar|floatformat:2 }} ₺
                    </td>
                </tr>
            </table>
        </div>
    </div>
</div>

<div class="card">
    <div class="card-header">
        <h2>Son Kasa Hareketleri</h2>
        <span class="text-muted">Son 20 hareket</span>
    </div>
    <div class="card-body">
        {% if son_hareketler %}
        <div class="table-wrap">
            <table class="data-table">
                <thead>
                    <tr>
                        <th>Tarih</th>
                        <th>Kasa</th>
                        <th>İşlem</th>
                        <th>Cari</th>
                        <th>Açıklama</th>
                        <th class="text-right">Tutar</th>
                    </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody>
                    {% for h in son_hareketler %}
                    <tr>
                        <td>{{ h.tarih|date:"d.m.Y" }}</td>
                        <td>{{ h.kasa.kasa_adi }}</td>
                        <td>
                            {% if h.islem_turu == 'giris' %}
                            <span class="badge badge-success">Giriş</span>
                            {% else %}
                            <span class="badge badge-danger">Çıkış</span>
                            {% endif %}
                        </td>
                        <td>{{ h.cari_hesap.unvan|default:"—" }}</td>
                        <td>{{ h.aciklama|default:"—"|truncatechars:60 }}</td>
                        <td class="text-right amount">{{ h.tutar|floatformat:2 }} ₺</td>
                    </tr>
                    {% endfor %}
                </tbody>
            </table>
        </div>
        {% else %}
        <div class="empty-state">
            <p>Henüz kasa hareketi bulunmuyor.</p>
        </div>
        {% endif %}
    </div>
</div>
{% endblock %}
```

yonetim/static/yonetim/css/style.css
```
*,
*::before,
*::after {
    box-sizing: border-box;
    margin: 0;
    padding: 0;
}

:root {
    --sidebar-width: 240px;
    --sidebar-bg: #1e293b;
    --sidebar-text: #e2e8f0;
    --sidebar-hover: #334155;
    --sidebar-active: #3b82f6;
    --body-bg: #f1f5f9;
    --card-bg: #ffffff;
    --text-primary: #0f172a;
    --text-secondary: #64748b;
    --border-color: #e2e8f0;
    --success: #16a34a;
    --error: #dc2626;
    --primary: #2563eb;
    --primary-hover: #1d4ed8;
    --info: #0284c7;
    --radius: 8px;
    --shadow: 0 1px 3px rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.08);
}

html {
    font-size: 15px;
}

body {
    font-family: 'Segoe UI', system-ui, -apple-system, sans-serif;
    background: var(--body-bg);
    color: var(--text-primary);
    line-height: 1.5;
    min-height: 100vh;
}

a {
    color: var(--primary);
    text-decoration: none;
}

a:hover {
    text-decoration: underline;
}

.layout {
    display: flex;
    min-height: 100vh;
}

.sidebar {
    width: var(--sidebar-width);
    background: var(--sidebar-bg);
    color: var(--sidebar-text);
    position: fixed;
    top: 0;
    left: 0;
    bottom: 0;
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    z-index: 100;
}

.sidebar-brand {
    padding: 1.25rem 1.5rem;
    font-size: 1.15rem;
    font-weight: 700;
    border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08);
    letter-spacing: 0.02em;
}

.sidebar-brand span {
    color: #93c5fd;
}

.sidebar-nav {
    flex: 1;
    padding: 1rem 0.75rem;
    overflow-y: auto;
}

.sidebar-nav a {
    display: block;
    padding: 0.65rem 1rem;
    color: var(--sidebar-text);
    border-radius: var(--radius);
    margin-bottom: 0.25rem;
    font-weight: 500;
    text-decoration: none;
    transition: background 0.15s ease;
}

.sidebar-nav a:hover {
    background: var(--sidebar-hover);
    text-decoration: none;
}

.sidebar-nav a.active {
    background: var(--sidebar-active);
    color: #ffffff;
}

.main {
    margin-left: var(--sidebar-width);
    flex: 1;
    min-width: 0;
}

.main-header {
    background: var(--card-bg);
    border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border-color);
    padding: 1rem 2rem;
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: space-between;
    position: sticky;
    top: 0;
    z-index: 50;
}

.main-header h1 {
    font-size: 1.35rem;
    font-weight: 600;
}

.header-actions {
    display: flex;
    gap: 0.5rem;
}

.main-content {
    padding: 1.5rem 2rem 2.5rem;
}

.btn {
    display: inline-flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
    padding: 0.5rem 1rem;
    border-radius: var(--radius);
    font-size: 0.9rem;
    font-weight: 600;
    border: none;
    cursor: pointer;
    text-decoration: none;
    transition: background 0.15s ease;
}

.btn:hover {
    text-decoration: none;
}

.btn-primary {
    background: var(--primary);
    color: #ffffff;
}

.btn-primary:hover {
    background: var(--primary-hover);
    color: #ffffff;
}

.btn-secondary {
    background: var(--card-bg);
    color: var(--text-primary);
    border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
}

.btn-secondary:hover {
    background: var(--body-bg);
    color: var(--text-primary);
}

.btn-mini {
    padding: 0.2rem 0.55rem;
    font-size: 0.72rem;
    font-weight: 600;
    background: var(--primary);
    color: #ffffff;
    border-radius: 4px;
}

.btn-mini:hover {
    background: var(--primary-hover);
    color: #ffffff;
}

.card {
    background: var(--card-bg);
    border-radius: var(--radius);
    box-shadow: var(--shadow);
    border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
    overflow: hidden;
}

.card-header {
    padding: 1rem 1.25rem;
    border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border-color);
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: space-between;
    gap: 1rem;
}

.card-header h2 {
    font-size: 1rem;
    font-weight: 600;
}

.card-body {
    padding: 0;
}

.table-wrap {
    overflow-x: auto;
}

table.data-table {
    width: 100%;
    border-collapse: collapse;
}

table.data-table th,
table.data-table td {
    padding: 0.75rem 1.25rem;
    text-align: left;
    border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border-color);
    vertical-align: middle;
}

table.data-table th {
    background: #f8fafc;
    font-size: 0.8rem;
    font-weight: 600;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    letter-spacing: 0.04em;
    color: var(--text-secondary);
    white-space: nowrap;
}

table.data-table tbody tr:hover {
    background: #f8fafc;
}

table.data-table tbody tr:last-child td {
    border-bottom: none;
}

.empty-state {
    padding: 2.5rem 1.25rem;
    text-align: center;
    color: var(--text-secondary);
}

.badge {
    display: inline-block;
    padding: 0.2rem 0.55rem;
    border-radius: 999px;
    font-size: 0.78rem;
    font-weight: 600;
}

.badge-success {
    background: #dcfce7;
    color: #166534;
}

.badge-warning {
    background: #fef9c3;
    color: #854d0e;
}

.badge-danger {
    background: #fee2e2;
    color: #991b1b;
}

.badge-info {
    background: #e0f2fe;
    color: #0369a1;
}

.badge-secondary {
    background: #f1f5f9;
    color: #475569;
}

.messages {
    margin-bottom: 1rem;
}

.message {
    padding: 0.75rem 1rem;
    border-radius: var(--radius);
    margin-bottom: 0.5rem;
    font-weight: 500;
}

.message-success {
    background: #dcfce7;
    color: #166534;
    border: 1px solid #bbf7d0;
}

.message-error {
    background: #fee2e2;
    color: #991b1b;
    border: 1px solid #fecaca;
}

.form-card {
    max-width: 680px;
}

.form-card-wide {
    max-width: 820px;
}

.form-grid {
    display: grid;
    gap: 1rem;
    padding: 1.25rem;
}

.form-row-2 {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr;
    gap: 1rem;
}

.form-group {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: 0.35rem;
}

.form-group label {
    font-size: 0.88rem;
    font-weight: 600;
    color: var(--text-primary);
}

.form-group input[type="text"],
.form-group input[type="number"],
.form-group input[type="date"],
.form-group input[type="file"],
.form-group input[type="email"],
.form-group select,
.form-group textarea {
    width: 100%;
    padding: 0.55rem 0.75rem;
    border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
    border-radius: var(--radius);
    font-size: 0.95rem;
    font-family: inherit;
    background: #ffffff;
    color: var(--text-primary);
}

.form-group textarea {
    min-height: 100px;
    resize: vertical;
}

.form-group input:focus,
.form-group select:focus,
.form-group textarea:focus {
    outline: none;
    border-color: var(--primary);
    box-shadow: 0 0 0 3px rgba(37, 99, 235, 0.15);
}

.form-actions {
    display: flex;
    gap: 0.75rem;
    padding: 0 1.25rem 1.25rem;
}

.thumbnail {
    width: 48px;
    height: 48px;
    object-fit: cover;
    border-radius: 4px;
    border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
}

.text-muted {
    color: var(--text-secondary);
    font-size: 0.88rem;
}

.text-right {
    text-align: right;
}

.amount {
    font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
    font-weight: 600;
}

.text-success {
    color: var(--success);
}

.text-danger {
    color: var(--error);
}

.field-hint {
    font-size: 0.8rem;
    color: var(--text-secondary);
}

.checkbox-label {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 0.5rem;
    font-weight: 600;
    cursor: pointer;
}

.checkbox-label input[type="checkbox"] {
    width: 1rem;
    height: 1rem;
}

.summary-grid {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: repeat(4, 1fr);
    gap: 1rem;
    margin-bottom: 1.5rem;
}

.summary-card {
    background: var(--card-bg);
    border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
    border-radius: var(--radius);
    padding: 1.25rem;
    box-shadow: var(--shadow);
}

.summary-label {
    display: block;
    font-size: 0.82rem;
    color: var(--text-secondary);
    margin-bottom: 0.35rem;
    font-weight: 600;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    letter-spacing: 0.03em;
}

.summary-value {
    display: block;
    font-size: 1.35rem;
    font-weight: 700;
    font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
}

.summary-primary .summary-value {
    color: var(--primary);
}

.summary-success .summary-value {
    color: var(--success);
}

.summary-danger .summary-value {
    color: var(--error);
}

.summary-warning .summary-value {
    color: #ca8a04;
}

.calc-table-wrap {
    padding: 1rem 1.25rem;
}

.calc-table {
    width: 100%;
    max-width: 520px;
    border-collapse: collapse;
}

.calc-table td {
    padding: 0.55rem 0;
    border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border-color);
}

.calc-table tr:last-child td {
    border-bottom: none;
}

.calc-positive td:last-child {
    color: var(--success);
}

.calc-negative td:last-child {
    color: var(--error);
}

.calc-total td {
    padding-top: 0.85rem;
    font-size: 1.05rem;
}

.pending-grid {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: repeat(3, 1fr);
    gap: 1rem;
    padding: 1.25rem;
}

.pending-item {
    background: #f8fafc;
    border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
    border-radius: var(--radius);
    padding: 1rem;
}

.pending-label {
    display: block;
    font-size: 0.82rem;
    color: var(--text-secondary);
    margin-bottom: 0.35rem;
    font-weight: 600;
}

.pending-value {
    display: block;
    font-size: 1.15rem;
    font-weight: 700;
    font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
}

.info-body {
    padding: 1.25rem;
}

.info-list {
    margin: 0 0 1rem 1.25rem;
    color: var(--text-primary);
}

.info-list li {
    margin-bottom: 0.45rem;
}

/* ---------- Haftalık Takvim ---------- */

.calendar-header {
    flex-wrap: wrap;
    gap: 0.75rem;
}

.calendar-nav {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 0.75rem;
    flex-wrap: wrap;
}

.calendar-range {
    font-weight: 600;
    font-size: 0.95rem;
    color: var(--text-primary);
    padding: 0 0.5rem;
    white-space: nowrap;
}

.calendar-grid {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: repeat(7, minmax(0, 1fr));
    gap: 0.5rem;
    padding: 1rem;
    background: #f8fafc;
}

.calendar-day {
    background: var(--card-bg);
    border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
    border-radius: var(--radius);
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    min-height: 220px;
    overflow: hidden;
}

.calendar-day-today {
    border-color: var(--primary);
    box-shadow: 0 0 0 2px rgba(37, 99, 235, 0.15);
}

.calendar-day-header {
    padding: 0.55rem 0.75rem;
    background: #f1f5f9;
    border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border-color);
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: space-between;
    gap: 0.5rem;
}

.calendar-day-today .calendar-day-header {
    background: #dbeafe;
}

.calendar-day-name {
    font-size: 0.82rem;
    font-weight: 700;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    letter-spacing: 0.03em;
    color: var(--text-secondary);
}

.calendar-day-date {
    font-size: 0.82rem;
    font-weight: 600;
    color: var(--text-primary);
}

.calendar-day-body {
    padding: 0.5rem;
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: 0.5rem;
    flex: 1;
    overflow-y: auto;
}

.calendar-empty {
    color: #cbd5e1;
    text-align: center;
    font-size: 0.9rem;
    padding: 0.5rem 0;
}

.plan-card {
    background: #eff6ff;
    border: 1px solid #bfdbfe;
    border-left: 3px solid var(--primary);
    border-radius: 6px;
    padding: 0.5rem 0.6rem;
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: 0.35rem;
}

.plan-card-done {
    background: #f0fdf4;
    border-color: #bbf7d0;
    border-left-color: var(--success);
}

.plan-card-title {
    font-size: 0.85rem;
    font-weight: 600;
    color: var(--text-primary);
    word-break: break-word;
}

.plan-card-detail {
    font-size: 0.75rem;
    color: var(--text-secondary);
    word-break: break-word;
}

.plan-card-footer {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: space-between;
    gap: 0.35rem;
    flex-wrap: wrap;
}

.plan-card-form {
    margin: 0;
}

/* ---------- Finansal Portföy Dashboard ---------- */

.dashboard-grid {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: repeat(3, 1fr);
    gap: 1rem;
    margin-bottom: 1.5rem;
}

.dashboard-grid .card-body {
    padding: 1rem 1.25rem;
}

.dashboard-grid .calc-table {
    max-width: none;
}

@media (max-width: 1100px) {
    .calendar-grid {
        grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr));
    }

    .dashboard-grid {
        grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    }
}

@media (max-width: 768px) {
    .summary-grid {
        grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr;
    }

    .pending-grid,
    .form-row-2 {
        grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    }

    .calendar-grid {
        grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    }

    .sidebar {
        width: 100%;
        position: relative;
        height: auto;
    }

    .main {
        margin-left: 0;
    }

    .layout {
        flex-direction: column;
    }

    .main-header,
    .main-content {
        padding-left: 1rem;
        padding-right: 1rem;
    }
}

The issue is that `&` in HTML should be escaped as `&amp;`. Let me fix these.

In finans_portfoy.html line 9: "Toplam Kasa & Banka" → "Toplam Kasa &amp; Banka"
In base.html lines 19, 22: "Kasa & Banka" → "Kasa &amp; Banka", "Çek & Senet" → "Çek &amp; Senet"

</thinking-content-7bbeb8e1441453ad999a0bbba8a46d4b>

HTML'de `&` karakteri `&amp;` olarak kaçış (escape) edilmelidir. Aksi halde tarayıcı bunu geçersiz bir HTML entity başlangıcı olarak yorumlayabilir. İlgili satırları düzeltiyorum.

yonetim/templates/yonetim/finans_portfoy.html
```
{% extends 'yonetim/base.html' %}

{% block title %}Finansal Portföy | Şirket Yönetim Paneli{% endblock %}
{% block page_title %}Finansal Portföy{% endblock %}

{% block content %}
<div class="summary-grid">
    <div class="summary-card summary-primary">
        <span class="summary-label">Toplam Kasa &amp; Banka</span>
        <span class="summary-value">{{ toplam_kasa|floatformat:2 }} ₺</span>
    </div>
    <div class="summary-card summary-success">
        <span class="summary-label">Cari Alacak</span>
        <span class="summary-value">{{ toplam_alacak|floatformat:2 }} ₺</span>
    </div>
    <div class="summary-card summary-danger">
        <span class="summary-label">Cari Borç</span>
        <span class="summary-value">{{ toplam_borc|floatformat:2 }} ₺</span>
    </div>
    <div class="summary-card {% if operasyonel_kar >= 0 %}summary-success{% else %}summary-danger{% endif %}">
        <span class="summary-label">Operasyonel Kâr / Zarar</span>
        <span class="summary-value">{{ operasyonel_kar|floatformat:2 }} ₺</span>
    </div>
</div>

<div class="dashboard-grid">
    <div class="card">
        <div class="card-header">
            <h2>Cari Denge</h2>
        </div>
        <div class="card-body">
            <table class="calc-table">
                <tr>
                    <td>Toplam Alacak</td>
                    <td class="text-success">{{ toplam_alacak|floatformat:2 }} ₺</td>
                </tr>
                <tr>
                    <td>Toplam Borç</td>
                    <td class="text-danger">{{ toplam_borc|floatformat:2 }} ₺</td>
                </tr>
                <tr class="calc-total">
                    <td>Net Denge</td>
                    <td class="{% if net_cari >= 0 %}text-success{% else %}text-danger{% endif %}">
                        {{ net_cari|floatformat:2 }} ₺
                    </td>
                </tr>
            </table>
        </div>
    </div>

    <div class="card">
        <div class="card-header">
            <h2>Bekleyen Çek / Senet</h2>
        </div>
        <div class="card-body">
            <table class="calc-table">
                <tr>
                    <td>Alınan (Portföyde)</td>
                    <td class="text-success">{{ bekleyen_alinan|floatformat:2 }} ₺</td>
                </tr>
                <tr>
                    <td>Verilen (Portföyde)</td>
                    <td class="text-danger">{{ bekleyen_verilen|floatformat:2 }} ₺</td>
                </tr>
                <tr class="calc-total">
                    <td>Net</td>
                    <td class="{% if net_ceksenet >= 0 %}text-success{% else %}text-danger{% endif %}">
                        {{ net_ceksenet|floatformat:2 }} ₺
                    </td>
                </tr>
            </table>
        </div>
    </div>

    <div class="card">
        <div class="card-header">
            <h2>Operasyonel Kâr / Zarar</h2>
        </div>
        <div class="card-body">
            <table class="calc-table">
                <tr>
                    <td>Gelen Faturalar</td>
                    <td class="text-success">{{ toplam_gelen_fatura|floatformat:2 }} ₺</td>
                </tr>
                <tr>
                    <td>Giden Faturalar</td>
                    <td class="text-danger">{{ toplam_giden_fatura|floatformat:2 }} ₺</td>
                </tr>
                <tr>
                    <td>İşletme Giderleri</td>
                    <td class="text-danger">{{ toplam_gider|floatformat:2 }} ₺</td>
                </tr>
                <tr class="calc-total">
                    <td>Net Kâr / Zarar</td>
                    <td class="{% if operasyonel_kar >= 0 %}text-success{% else %}text-danger{% endif %}">
                        {{ operasyonel_kar|floatformat:2 }} ₺
                    </td>
                </tr>
            </table>
        </div>
    </div>
</div>

<div class="card">
    <div class="card-header">
        <h2>Son Kasa Hareketleri</h2>
        <span class="text-muted">Son 20 hareket</span>
    </div>
    <div class="card-body">
        {% if son_hareketler %}
        <div class="table-wrap">
            <table class="data-table">
                <thead>
                    <tr>
                        <th>Tarih</th>
                        <th>Kasa</th>
                        <th>İşlem</th>
                        <th>Cari</th>
                        <th>Açıklama</th>
                        <th class="text-right">Tutar</th>
                    </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody>
                    {% for h in son_hareketler %}
                    <tr>
                        <td>{{ h.tarih|date:"d.m.Y" }}</td>
                        <td>{{ h.kasa.kasa_adi }}</td>
                        <td>
                            {% if h.islem_turu == 'giris' %}
                            <span class="badge badge-success">Giriş</span>
                            {% else %}
                            <span class="badge badge-danger">Çıkış</span>
                            {% endif %}
                        </td>
                        <td>{{ h.cari_hesap.unvan|default:"—" }}</td>
                        <td>{{ h.aciklama|default:"—"|truncatechars:60 }}</td>
                        <td class="text-right amount">{{ h.tutar|floatformat:2 }} ₺</td>
                    </tr>
                    {% endfor %}
                </tbody>
            </table>
        </div>
        {% else %}
        <div class="empty-state">
            <p>Henüz kasa hareketi bulunmuyor.</p>
        </div>
        {% endif %}
    </div>
</div>
{% endblock %}
```

yonetim/templates/yonetim/base.html
```
{% load static %}
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="tr">
<head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8">
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
    <title>{% block title %}Şirket Yönetim Paneli{% endblock %}</title>
    <link rel="stylesheet" href="{% static 'yonetim/css/style.css' %}">
</head>
<body>
    <div class="layout">
        <aside class="sidebar">
            <div class="sidebar-brand">
                Şirket<span>Panel</span>
            </div>
            <nav class="sidebar-nav">
                <a href="{% url 'yonetim:finans_portfoy' %}" class="{% if active_menu == 'portfoy' %}active{% endif %}">Finansal Portföy</a>
                <a href="{% url 'yonetim:cari_liste' %}" class="{% if active_menu == 'cariler' %}active{% endif %}">Cari Hesaplar</a>
                <a href="{% url 'yonetim:kasa_liste' %}" class="{% if active_menu == 'kasalar' %}active{% endif %}">Kasa &amp; Banka</a>
                <a href="{% url 'yonetim:gider_liste' %}" class="{% if active_menu == 'giderler' %}active{% endif %}">İşletme Giderleri</a>
                <a href="{% url 'yonetim:fatura_liste' %}" class="{% if active_menu == 'faturalar' %}active{% endif %}">Faturalar</a>
                <a href="{% url 'yonetim:ceksenet_liste' %}" class="{% if active_menu == 'ceksenet' %}active{% endif %}">Çek &amp; Senet</a>
                <a href="{% url 'yonetim:odeme_liste' %}" class="{% if active_menu == 'odemeler' %}active{% endif %}">Ödemeler</a>
                <a href="{% url 'yonetim:alacak_liste' %}" class="{% if active_menu == 'alacaklar' %}active{% endif %}">Alacaklar</a>
                <a href="{% url 'yonetim:haftalik_plan_liste' %}" class="{% if active_menu == 'planlar' %}active{% endif %}">Haftalık Takvim</a>
                <a href="{% url 'yonetim:stok_liste' %}" class="{% if active_menu == 'stoklar' %}active{% endif %}">Stoklar</a>
                <a href="{% url 'yonetim:butce_liste' %}" class="{% if active_menu == 'butce' %}active{% endif %}">Bütçe</a>
                <a href="{% url 'yonetim:vergi_ayarlari' %}" class="{% if active_menu == 'vergilendirme' %}active{% endif %}">Vergilendirme</a>
                <a href="{% url 'yonetim:fis_liste' %}" class="{% if active_menu == 'fisler' %}active{% endif %}">Fişler</a>
            </nav>
        </aside>

        <div class="main">
            <header class="main-header">
                <h1>{% block page_title %}Panel{% endblock %}</h1>
                {% block header_actions %}{% endblock %}
            </header>

            <main class="main-content">
                {% if messages %}
                <div class="messages">
                    {% for message in messages %}
                    <div class="message message-{{ message.tags }}">{{ message }}</div>
                    {% endfor %}
                </div>
                {% endif %}

                {% block content %}{% endblock %}
            </main>
        </div>
    </div>
</body>
</html>
```

"""
Django settings for sirket_panel project.
"""

import os
from pathlib import Path

import dj_database_url

BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent

SECRET_KEY = os.environ.get(
    'SECRET_KEY',
    'django-insecure-sirket-panel-dev-key-degistirin',
)

CSRF_TRUSTED_ORIGINS = [
    'https://*.onrender.com',
]

INSTALLED_APPS = [

    'django.contrib.contenttypes',

build.sh
```
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -o errexit

pip install -r requirements.txt
python manage.py collectstatic --no-input
python manage.py migrate
```

render.yaml
```
services:
  - type: web
    name: sirket-yonetim-paneli
    env: python
    buildCommand: "./build.sh"
    startCommand: "gunicorn sirket_panel.wsgi:application"
    envVars:
      - key: PYTHON_VERSION

### Yapılan değişikliklerin özeti

- **requirements.txt**: `psycopg2-binary`, `dj-database-url`, `gunicorn` ve `whitenoise` eklendi.
- **settings.py**:

  - `ALLOWED_HOSTS = ['*']` yapıldı ve Render için `CSRF_TRUSTED_ORIGINS` eklendi.
  - `DATABASES` artık `DATABASE_URL` ortam değişkeninden okunuyor; tanımlı değilse verdiğiniz Neon bağlantı adresi kullanılıyor. `ssl_require=True` ile SSL zorunlu kılındı.
  - `MIDDLEWARE` içine `SecurityMiddleware`'in hemen altına `WhiteNoiseMiddleware` eklendi.

### Dikkat edilmesi gerekenler

1. **`build.sh` çalıştırma izni**: Render'da `build.sh`'ın çalışabilmesi için git'te executable olması gerekir. Yerelde şu komutu çalıştırın:
   ```bash
   chmod +x build.sh
   git update-index --chmod=+x build.sh
   ```
2. **Güvenlik**: Neon bağlantı adresi ve şifre hem `settings.py` içinde fallback olarak hem de `render.yaml` içinde açık şekilde yer alıyor. Bu bilgiler git deposuna gireceği için **herkese açık hale gelir**. Üretimde bu değerleri Render panelinden "Environment Variables" olarak tanımlamanız ve `render.yaml`'dan kaldırmanız önerilir. Ayrıca Neon panelinden bu şifreyi rotate etmenizi tavsiye ederim.
3. **`SECRET_KEY`**: Üretimde `SECRET_KEY` ortam değişkenini Render'da tanımlayın; varsayılan geliştirme anahtarı güvensizdir.
4. **`DEBUG`**: Render'da `DEBUG=False` ortam değişkenini tanımlamayı unutmayın.
